<span class="ct-node-label">Cryptozoology</span>
<span class="ct-node-title-full">Cryptids, Evidence, and the Animals We Imagine</span>
<span class="ct-node-summary">Cryptozoology is the search for animals whose existence has been reported but not confirmed by mainstream zoology: Bigfoot, the Loch Ness Monster, the Yeti, sea serpents, surviving “prehistoric” creatures and other disputed animals often called cryptids. Its appeal is easy to understand.</span>
</span>
</a>
